TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -from [get_clocks {CLK_27M}] -to [get_clocks {CLK_24M}]
TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -from [get_clocks {CLK_27M}] -to [get_clocks {CLK_PLL}]
TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -from [get_clocks {CLK_24M}] -to [get_clocks {CLK_27M}]
TC_FALSE_PATH Actived set_false_path -from [get_clocks {CLK_PLL}] -to [get_clocks {CLK_27M}]
